Their leaves (`drained_hole_is_a_prison`, `toss_flame_from_rim`, `raiders_fetch_their_fallen`) are owed code, not owed tests. (`inv_irrigation`, `unknown` at the lock, left for 0.2.0.)
- 2026-11-16T07:16:23Z BACKFILL#82 trap.noted trap:every-live-scene-s-tier-says-quicktest v0.1.0: Every live scene's tier says "quicktest", but the `tier_reason` of 25 of 31 leaves is one boiler-plate sentence ("an on-screen look and a live state change: needs a running map"). Whether a faster test decides each is the Stage 4 challenger's question; the reason is written, not shown true.
- 2026-11-16T07:16:23Z BACKFILL#83 trap.noted trap:the-c-selftest-is-one-console-app-holding v0.1.0: The C# SelfTest is one console app holding most of the offline checks (325+ cases); a Python wrapper `src/RimMandrake/Utils/selftest_flowworks_stock.py` builds it with Windows `dotnet.exe`. One run covers many bars.
- 2026-11-16T07:16:23Z BACKFILL#84 trap.noted trap:the-stage-3-offline-checks-of-five-bars v0.1.0: The Stage 3 offline checks of five bars are source laws, not behaviour runs (`selftest_stage3_owed.py`): the code is Verse-bound. They decide "the code that would make the bar true exists and has this shape"; whether it works is the scene's question at Stage 6.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#94 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_CONVERTER_RATE_REMAINDER_EATEN_BY_THE_CAP v0.1.0: A converter whose income beats a batch loses the part-batch remainder to the cap, running short of its rate: seed 4242 ate 262,080 of about 267,207 in 3 days; worst 50% short. Detox Works at the 2x slider runs 40% short, Desal Plant 20% short; none at 1x.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#95 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_CONVERTER_RATE_REMAINDER_EATEN_BY_THE_CAP v0.1.0: RM_ConversionMath.CapAccrued now caps at perTick + inputUnits when income exceeds a batch, so the part-batch remainder carries instead of being clipped.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#96 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_CONVERTER_CALLER_KEEPS_CLIPPED_INCOME v0.1.0: Planted CALLER_CONV_DROPS_CLIPPED (accrued += clipped changed to += 0f in CompLiquidConverter) survived T_CONVERT and lane 1, so T_CONVERT could not fail when the caller drops clipped income.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#97 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_CONVERTER_CALLER_KEEPS_CLIPPED_INCOME v0.1.0: Added the source-law case Converter_caller_keeps_clipped_income to T_CONVERT; the planted fault is now caught (rc 0), where before it was MISSED (rc 1).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#98 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_SHEET_DRY_ISLAND_UNDER_A_STANDING_POOL v0.1.0: In a closed 25-square yard with 3 cm of water, one square (cell 36, relief +119 dL) stays dry for good while neighbours' surface is 604 dL, so water stands 2.4 cm above its ground beside it; the sheet does not lie level.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#99 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_SHEET_DRY_ISLAND_UNDER_A_STANDING_POOL v0.1.0: RM_SheetMath.Sweep now gives a dry receiver its minimum when diff/2 reaches it. The cause was Sweep's flux diff/5 = 97 dL falling under MinReceiveDl 100 while Level never adds a dry square.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#100 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_SPRINKLER_CALLER_TO_A_WORKING_PUMP v0.1.0: A planted fault, CALLER_SPR_SPRAYS_WITHOUT_PUMP (pressure = enabled && switchOn, no HasPressure), survived T_SPRINKLER and lane 1, so no test failed when the sprinkler caller stopped asking for a pump.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#101 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_SPRINKLER_CALLER_TO_A_WORKING_PUMP v0.1.0: Added the T_SPRINKLER case Sprinkler_caller_asks_for_a_pump, which goes red when the sprinkler caller drops its pump (HasPressure) requirement; the planted fault is now caught by it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#102 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_DRILL_DECIDER_TO_ITS_LANDED_CHARGE v0.1.0: Planted DRILL_CHARGES_WHOLE_LEVEL (SpentUnits rounds up to a whole level) survived T_DRILL and was caught only by lane 1, so T_DRILL could not fail when a drill is charged for liquid that never landed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#103 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_DRILL_DECIDER_TO_ITS_LANDED_CHARGE v0.1.0: Added DrillSurvey_charge_is_only_what_landed inside T_DRILL's glob; the planted whole-level charge is now caught by T_DRILL.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#104 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_REPIN_PIT_COVER_CHECK_PAST_GSS_NEW_PARAMETER v0.1.0: T_PIT_COVER FAILs (recorded PASS): DeckCollapse_GSS_shock_seam_is_soft_and_load_safe pinned the whole old RebuildContacts signature as source text, and Gimme Some Slack 63ac285403 added an optional HashSet<int> dropped.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#105 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_REPIN_PIT_COVER_CHECK_PAST_GSS_NEW_PARAMETER v0.1.0: cf6efc3055 re-pinned the DeckCollapse GSS seam check so it follows the four parameters FlowWorks relies on, tolerating the new optional RebuildContacts parameter.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#106 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_TRIM_SPRINKLER_VALVE_SHORT_ON_EXACT_FIT v0.1.0: MaxCellsFor(7 units, 0.1 L, 10) returned 6 though 7 cells cost exactly 7; float 0.1f x 10 = 1.0000000149 made the thirst valve water one cell too few on exact fits.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#107 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_TRIM_SPRINKLER_VALVE_SHORT_ON_EXACT_FIT v0.1.0: MaxCellsFor now steps n up against UnitsFor after the floor, fixing the float rounding (0.1f x 10 = 1.0000000149) that gave 6 for 7 units at exact fit. Fixed at 4b37a37db9.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#108 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_CORRECT_SPRINKLER_RADIUS_TEXT_SAYS_TWENTY_FIVE v0.1.0: The settings label said 'default 2.9: about 29 squares watered' and the kernel comment said '29 cells'. RM_Sprinkle.Covered uses GenRadial.RadialCellsAround, which counts distance <= 2.9, i.e. 25 cells.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#109 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIMMANDRAKE_CORRECT_SPRINKLER_RADIUS_TEXT_SAYS_TWENTY_FIVE v0.1.0: Label and kernel comment corrected to 25 cells, matching what GenRadial.RadialCellsAround counts at radius 2.9.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#110 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_ANCHOR_CROP_DROWN_DELAY_IN_DAYS_NOT_PULSES v0.1.0: cropDrownPulses ships 1680 = 7 days at the shipped 250-tick pulse, but it is stored in pulses, so moving the pulse slider (60..2500) changes the delay to 1.68..70 days.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:14Z BACKFILL#111 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PIN_MACHINE_RATES_A_TENFOLD_STILL_PASSES v0.1.0: Planted DEF_STILL_RATE_TENFOLD (RM_LiquidStills.xml 6000 changed to 60000 L a day) survived every decider; the rates are marked PROVISIONAL and no bar states them.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#112 finding.open finding:ABSOLUTE_DESCENT_FIX_MISSES_DECK_SHOVE v0.1.0: With pitRelativeClimbEnabled off, IsFallingArrival compares raw ExcavatedDepthAt(prev) and ExcavatedDepthAt(pos). A pawn shoved from a deck over a superdeep excavation into an uncovered square of the same excavation reads 4->4, so no descent: no fall damage, no spike descent event.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#113 finding.open finding:ALL_OFF_FIX_DEPTH_ENGINE_STAYS_ON v0.1.0: _setting_in writes depthEngineEnabled=False without depthEngineLiveForTests, so on a normally loaded game the engine stays on. The all_off fixture is not all off and all_off_still_digs can report a false mod failure.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#114 finding.open finding:AUTO_SPLICE_FIX_SKIPS_OBSTACLE_CHECK v0.1.0: RefreshMates calls MateTail with blocked=null, so the automatic splice join between two open hose ends can be drawn through wall gaps. The splice API has no max-length input, so a join can exceed the reel's capacity. Render only.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#115 trap.noted trap:the-census-wall-and-length-measurements-of-the v0.1.0: The census wall and length measurements of the original HoseLay can stay clean while the splice join passes through walls or exceeds length, because splice changes are draw-only.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#116 finding.open finding:BIGNET_PROOF_FIX_SIZE_NOT_RESTORED v0.1.0: In flow_bignet_live.py only inland is restored from saved state. Resumed dig and pulse phases use the default --size 80 while the cell count comes from st["size"], so the proof can PASS without digging or wetting the intended far corner.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#117 trap.noted trap:after-site-size-100-dig-pulse-without-size v0.1.0: After `site --size 100`, dig/pulse without --size run on 80 while the result reports 10,000 cells from saved state; a PASS does not show the far corner was dug or wetted.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#118 finding.open finding:CONCEALED_COVER_FIX_SHIELDS_FROM_LIQUID v0.1.0: UnderDeck recognizes only suspension floor-band pawns (InFloorBand). A pawn beneath a solid concealed cover is IsBeneath but gets SplashShare 0, so RM_PitFillEffects.Tick skips both poisoning and drowning.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#119 finding.claimed_fixed finding:CONCEALED_COVER_FIX_SHIELDS_FROM_LIQUID v0.1.0: Fixed in source (e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358)): a concealed cover no longer shields the pawn beneath it from the fill; selftest red-then-green.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#120 finding.open finding:COVER_BLAST_FIX_DECK_LEFT_INTACT v0.1.0: A blast that kills a cover before the explosion postfix runs leaves the rest of the connected deck standing. DeSpawn drops only that cell's riders, and the postfix then finds no cover. A weaker blast that leaves the first cover alive springs the whole deck.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#121 finding.open finding:DEEP_BEDS_FIX_FLOOR_STOPS_AT_FOUR v0.1.0: A painted 5184-square lake with deep beds on read deepest=4, bedSum=19080, where the 2026-10-22 belt read 12. DL_lake_over_4000_is_limited and DL_drained_reads_gradual_hollow FAILED and both colonist rows were UNMEASURED.

RecordLakeFloor ran (AnyMovingWater false); the seeded R11 draw was silted, 'deepest 2 m', and lakeDepthScale scales only the excess over 2 m. Result is SITE.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#125 finding.open finding:DEEP_SAND_FIX_READS_AS_FRESHWATER v0.1.0: RM_DeepSand carries a Freshwater body and Water tag (for fishing), so it counts as fresh water for drinking (IsWater && !IsOcean) and as a pond source accepted by the excavation. Pumps and canals beside it treat sand as natural liquid.

An RM_DigCanal designation was added at a depth-3 square, depth stayed [3,3], DIGFINDS 0 before/after.

The order is accepted and hasJobOnCell RM_DigCanalJob is logged, but depth still stayed 3 in 6000 ticks, so the cut row is not yet measured. Depth stayed 3 over 6000 ticks, so A1 is not verified.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#132 finding.open finding:DEEP_STONE_PROOF_FIX_RERUN_LETTER_FAILS v0.1.0: deep_stone_live.py:176-177 requires a new 'Deep stone' first-find letter. RM_DigDiscovery.cs:161 found.Add fires once per def per map, so a correct rerun without --fresh-map has no second letter and reports MOD FAIL.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#133 trap.noted trap:a-mod-fail-on-a-rerun-without-fresh v0.1.0: A MOD FAIL on a rerun without --fresh-map is not a mod fault. The cut and chunk succeed, and the letter is withheld because the map remembers the chunk was already discovered. The rectangle reset in RM_NorthstarProofs.cs clears rolled depths, not the discovery set.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#134 finding.open finding:DEPTH_PROOFS_FIX_EXIT_ZERO_ON_FAIL v0.1.0: depth_rebuild_proofs.py:526 `main` returns 0 regardless of FAIL rows; only line 163 returns 2. A gate reading the exit code sees success after failed proofs, e.g. `--only p5` with a wrong liquid total or a nonlevel surface.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#135 trap.noted trap:exit-code-0-from-depth-rebuild-proofs-py v0.1.0: Exit code 0 from depth_rebuild_proofs.py does not mean the proofs passed. Read the JSON rows for FAIL.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:15Z BACKFILL#136 finding.open finding:DIG_FINDS_PROVE_NEVER_FINISHES_ON_STEVE v0.1.0: The dig_finds chain (finds_off_none, cut_turns_up_local_find, first_find_sends_letter) fails on SteveDesktop with 'ExpectationFailed: the dig job never finished' in all 29 fails from 2026-10-26 on. The standalone quarry_find_live.py A5 passed 2026-10-22.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#137 trap.noted trap:the-feature-is-broken-is-not-supported-the v0.1.0: 'The feature is broken' is not supported: the same chain passed on a dry site 2026-10-15 and standalone A5 passed 2026-10-22.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#138 trap.noted trap:the-river-map-is-the-cause-is-not v0.1.0: 'The river map is the cause' is not supported: it spoiled only the 2026-10-15 R3 runs, while the failures are on the dry-site chain.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#139 trap.noted trap:the-labour-multiplier-theory-cuts-of-7-529 v0.1.0: The labour-multiplier theory (cuts of 7,529 / 15,059 / 22,588 ticks against an 8,000-tick helper window and a 4,000-tick standalone wait) is derived from code arithmetic only. MiningSpeed and mining skill were never measured on Steve. Do not treat the tick figures as measured.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#140 trap.noted trap:first-find-sends-letter-has-never-been-read v0.1.0: first_find_sends_letter has never been read. A rock chunk sends no letter by design, so the row needs a forced non-chunk find through RM_DigDiscoveryProof.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#141 trap.noted trap:passing-shas-ee9fa61fbc-and-the-lesson-s-13ba79077b v0.1.0: Passing shas ee9fa61fbc and the lesson's 13ba79077b do not resolve in this clone. Passes of 2026-10-15 and 10-22 predate the x4 multiplier from ac3aab2458 (2026-10-25).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#142 trap.noted trap:do-not-count-the-invalid-harness-runs-modcheck v0.1.0: Do not count the INVALID/HARNESS runs (modcheck exit 5, companion_handshake, REFUSED/SYNC, bridge programState error) as feature failures. The 77 partial verifies are unmeasured: the events carry only the sheet path, not the row.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#143 finding.open finding:DRAWBRIDGE_BUILD_MOAT_WITH_MANNERS v0.1.0: Build the ruled FlowWorks drawbridge (timber hand-winch, steel powered): bascule leaf, gate leaf and plank art. Raising tips riders to the hinge bank without Collapse. Peace mode auto-lowers and any free hostile locks it. Raiders get trench rules, winch sappers and raid planks.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#144 trap.noted trap:drawbridge-art-is-judged-in-game-only-drawbridge v0.1.0: Drawbridge art is judged in game only. drawbridge_art_sheet_2026-10-20 is retired and 35 held art rows stay uninstalled until the owner's look.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#145 trap.noted trap:deconstructing-a-winch-while-its-leaf-is-up v0.1.0: Deconstructing a winch while its leaf is up CRASHES the leaf (the general rule 'winch destroyed -> crash'). The alternative, lower-then-release, is unruled.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#146 finding.open finding:DRAWBRIDGE_LOWER_FIX_LIFTS_OCCUPANTS_BENEATH v0.1.0: Lowering a drawbridge leaf restores the deck cells without classifying pawns already on the floor beneath it into the floor band. They then read as on the deck: draw height, load, immersion protection and movement band change.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#147 trap.noted trap:while-the-leaf-is-raised-there-is-no v0.1.0: While the leaf is raised there is no passing deck, so pawns on the exposed floor carry no floor-band flag, and SweepUnder also clears existing flags. A pawn that stays put through the lowering therefore ends up on the deck.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#148 finding.open finding:DROWNING_ESCAPE_FIX_READS_DECK_ABOVE v0.1.0: RM_Drowning.ExitInReach reads StepDepthAt of the decked square at deck height, so a floor-band pawn under a bridge is judged able to step to the undug bank and gets no drowning severity.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#149 finding.open finding:FILL_TERRAIN_FIX_HAZARDS_DRUNK_AS_WATER v0.1.0: Hazardous canal fill terrains (e.g. chemfuel) carry Water/WaterFreshShallow tags and no terrainSuite registers them, so thirst falls back to fresh water and pawns drink chemfuel, propane, poison or boiling water as potable, with the canal quantity unchanged.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#150 finding.claimed_fixed finding:FILL_TERRAIN_FIX_HAZARDS_DRUNK_AS_WATER v0.1.0: 24 hazard fill terrains now use the inert RM_Liquid_NotDrinkable terrain, so they are no longer drunk as fresh water; selftest green. (recorded on pre-landing bd36286c4795; landed as 015b7892e833)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#151 trap.noted trap:the-live-criterion-a1-thirsty-pawn-does-not v0.1.0: The live criterion A1 (thirsty pawn does not drink hazard fill) was filed but only the selftest was recorded green; the live look has not been recorded.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#152 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_BARS_JUDGE_FIVE_IN_ONE_SITTING v0.1.0: The FlowWorks north-star section must be re-validated by the owner at a new hash: new bar still_water_one_surface_height plus reworded fill_tier_legible, depth_and_fill_jointly_legible, pit_depth_ladder_legible and pawn_height_ladder_legible. It stays DRAFT, with no hash.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#153 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_CHECKOUT_SCOPE_1 v0.1.0: The FlowWorks walk's step 4 (doors, spikes, ladders, prison room, pump, bottles, shores, fire etc.) sat outside the basic checkout, so a green core proof said nothing about them. Underlying-terrain recovery and flood expiry have no live row.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#154 cause.refuted cause:the-walk-claimed-the-flood-never-calls-setterrain v0.1.0: The walk claimed the flood 'never' calls SetTerrain; refuted: CoolFrontToGlass calls it by design for coolsToGlassEdge fluids (tar glass).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#155 trap.noted trap:every-extension-result-json-on-disk-predates-the v0.1.0: Every extension_result_*.json on disk predates the recording contract, so `--extensions` refuses each one; a full step-4 rerun right after a core run is required.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#156 trap.noted trap:a-green-entry-with-no-step-4-record v0.1.0: A GREEN entry with no step-4 record (e.g. a `modcheck run`, or any pre-contract entry) reads step4 UNMEASURED, not PASS; only a stored PASS at the current mod hash counts.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#157 trap.noted trap:modcheck-record-refused-the-2026-10-12-step v0.1.0: modcheck record refused the 2026-10-12 step-4 results as STALE because FlowWorks source moved during the sitting. Run step 4 and record in one sitting with no code edits (~1h run).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#158 trap.noted trap:underlying-terrain-recovery-after-a-flood-and-flood v0.1.0: Underlying-terrain recovery after a flood and flood expiry at 2×FloodingTicks are UNCOVERED live: no row or chain spawns a Flood_FlowWorks release, so the green core proof does not cover them.

The old names match nothing, a silent no-op.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#160 trap.noted trap:the-stale-rsw-names-raise-no-error-the v0.1.0: The stale RSW_ names raise no error: the patch matches nothing, so the ladder-climber behavior is silently missing for the moved creatures. More names (Excretor, SandStalker, VentStalker, Onnik ...) follow with later batches.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#161 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_CODE_REVIEW_FINISH_DIRTY_FILES v0.1.0: A FlowWorks code review begun by FOUNDRY helpers (a 41-file wave) was left unfinished; 67 tracked FlowWorks .cs/.py/.xml files were not CLEAN at filing.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#162 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_CONTAINER_MATERIALS_1 v0.1.0: Bottles, barrels and buckets must be made from a player-chosen material (bottle: leather/glass from any stone/metal; barrel: wood/metal/plasteel; bucket: wood/metal/leather), made at the stations that suit each material.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#164 trap.noted trap:ruling-2-s-first-wording-says-glass-and v0.1.0: Ruling 2's first wording says glass and metal can hold acid. The final acid table is: glass and plasteel hold it; leather, wood and metal refuse. Only leather refuses hot liquids; wood buckets and barrels hold hot liquids.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#165 trap.noted trap:review-map-py-spawns-rm-bottleempty-and-rm v0.1.0: review_map.py spawns RM_BottleEmpty and RM_BarrelEmpty with no stuff, so the vanilla 'made from stuff but no stuff' error may fire there. Old saves with unstuffed containers get a default stuff plus a one-time error.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#166 trap.noted trap:the-progress-log-transient-flowworks-container-materials-progress v0.1.0: The progress log Transient/flowworks_container_materials_progress_2026-10-06.md is gone from disk. Its last copy is in git at a9c743392e.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#167 trap.noted trap:a-1-unit-bottle-only-moves-liquid-at v0.1.0: A 1-unit bottle only moves liquid at a capacity factor >= 1.5, so the bottle capacity column reads 1/1/1 for leather, glass and metal.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:16Z BACKFILL#168 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_DEPTH_REBUILD_ONE_BIG_DIG v0.1.0: Epic: FlowWorks water is not exactly level and digs stop at 2 m. It must deliver decilitre stock, one surface per connected body (within 1 cm), a very-shallow look under 25 cm, 8 m default dig (2-20 m setting), and relative drowning/fall/spike/shooting rules.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#169 trap.noted trap:level-surface-bar-py-is-a-stub-today v0.1.0: level_surface_bar.py is a stub today; it only becomes a real check once built on P3's per-body surface-gap diagnostic. Until then the level bar is not measured.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#173 trap.noted trap:the-first-look-and-bars2-shots-were-all v0.1.0: The first_look and bars2 shots were all taken at rootSize 7.2 (~64 px a cell), closer than a player's floor of root 11, so they do not show what a player sees. The seam-at-max-zoom TRUE also contradicted PROGRESS, which said built but not deployed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#174 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_FIX_NATIVE_WATER_AUDIT_RED v0.1.0: The natives-drink-own-water audit is red. RM_Miasma brackish deep and shallow read NotDrinkable, and RM_TheChill RM_PropaneDeep (Propane) reads BLOCKED.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#175 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_GUARDS_FIX_THREE_RED_SELFTESTS v0.1.0: Three selftests are red: selftest_static_game_state (HosePorts.cs:264 sideIds and RM_WallReach.cs:199/202 kindMap/kindEx unreviewed), selftest_hose_offline HO7 (B4 port cache uses CacheFresh), and selftest_flowworks_review1022_liquidnet (outside origin row).

A gate red means another offline step.

Each needs tracing to MOD, SITE or HARNESS.

Later targeted runs pass, except pump_pours_back intermittently fails (pour mode 3.00->3.00 / 4.00->4.00), tracked in FLOWWORKS_PUMP_FIX_POURS_BACK_STILL_THREE.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#183 trap.noted trap:a-planned-full-sweep-needs-16740-s-but v0.1.0: A planned full sweep needs 16740 s but the worker caps at 7200 s, so late chains read UNMEASURED rather than failing.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#185 finding.open finding:FLOWWORKS_NORTHSTAR_GREEN_MINIMAL_1 v0.1.0: FlowWorks north star must reach GREEN on the minimal flowworks tier with a clean preflight, and the owner must review the sheet once.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:17Z BACKFILL#186 trap.noted trap:offline-p-o4-fail-is-not-a-flowworks v0.1.0: Offline P-O4 FAIL is not a FlowWorks defect: the owner's pre-swap list is the 50-mod list, not FULL.LATEST (environment).

- 2026-11-16T08:32:18Z BACKFILL#194 trap.noted trap:no-saved-copy-of-the-639-mod-full v0.1.0: No saved copy of the 639-mod full list exists (bridge4 rebuilt it ad hoc), so GREEN_FULL cannot be reproduced yet.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:18Z BACKFILL#195 trap.noted trap:the-north-star-section-gained-still-water-one v0.1.0: The north-star section gained still_water_one_surface_height (R9) and needs the owner's re-validation before any GREEN means anything.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#275 finding.open finding:INDUSTRIAL_RUINS_FIX_HIDDEN_EH_DEPENDENCY v0.1.0: Every industrial ruin scatter has MayRequire mandrake.rm.environmentalhazards, which About.xml does not declare. With industrialWorksBuildAnywhere=false, FlowWorks without EH has no industrial works (desal, detox, tar refinery, pumping station).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#276 trap.noted trap:gpt-labelled-the-finding-major-but-verification-confirmed v0.1.0: GPT labelled the finding MAJOR but verification confirmed MINOR: the pack ships EH, so it only bites standalone FlowWorks.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#277 finding.open finding:JOINED_WEIR_BREACH_FIX_CATCH_UNTOUCHED v0.1.0: On a joined weir wall the catch is deposited at RunHead().BankCell but Breach washes HeldCatch() of the breaching section. A breach away from the head leaves the head's shared catch on the bank.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#278 finding.open finding:LADDER_PARITY_FIX_RAIDERS_SKIP_LEANING v0.1.0: RM_LadderParity.MightClimb admits only the player's faction or a ladder carrier, so a ladderless hostile never reaches CanClimbAt for an already leaning ladder. Raiders bash or stay outside instead of using it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#279 trap.noted trap:the-gpt-finding-text-says-severity-major-but v0.1.0: The GPT finding text says severity MAJOR, but the verification verdict and ticket title say MINOR.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:20Z BACKFILL#280 finding.open finding:LAKE_FLOOR_SELFTEST_FIX_WET_SKIPS_HEIGHT v0.1.0: LakeFloorChecks.EdgeReachable returns reachable on any wet cell before comparing heights, so a sunken lake with its surface nine notches below land passes. The drained-lake access proof can miss an inaccessible final descent.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#281 trap.noted trap:the-lake-access-selftest-ruler-can-report-a v0.1.0: The lake-access selftest ruler can report a sunken lake reachable: for a one-cell lake with floor depth 10 notches, surface -9*U and climb limit 1, it returns true. A green result does not prove access.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#282 finding.open finding:LAKE_FLOOR_STAGE_TAME_QUADRATIC_SETS v0.1.0: lake_floor_stage.py:114 builds set(cells) inside a 9-neighbour lambda for each candidate, giving about 9N^2 insertions on a large lake (a 10,000-cell lake needs about 900 million) and making natural-lake staging slow.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#283 finding.open finding:LAVA_LIQUID_BUILD_PROMISED_BY_CARD v0.1.0: The lava liquid the owner promoted by card 2026-09-24 was missing from the FlowWorks liquid registry. It is built at 3f6213543d1f; the Forge-map check A4 is still owed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#284 trap.noted trap:the-a3-pass-log-was-taken-at-0afba7de51 v0.1.0: The A3 pass log was taken at 0afba7de51, not the built sha 3f6213543d1f; the 7 config errors in it were pre-existing vanilla/RUT_Jawa, not lava. A3 shows only that the row loads without error, not that lava works in play; A4 is unmeasured.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#285 finding.open finding:LEVELLING_BUDGET_FIX_STARVES_LATER_HOSES v0.1.0: RM_Levelling.DugBody adds the whole run to WalkBudget before the keyIndex cache check, and hoses are iterated in fixed order. Early components re-walk known bodies and later hose components starve every cycle.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#286 trap.noted trap:the-rotating-cursor-applies-only-after-links-are v0.1.0: The rotating cursor applies only after links are constructed, so it cannot rescue omitted links; fairness must be fixed in the walk budget.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#287 finding.open finding:LEVEL_SET_SELFTEST_FIX_IDLE_PULSE_SETTLES v0.1.0: LevelSetChecks.Run records settled as the last changed pulse, so a move/idle oscillation returns 299 of 300 rather than -1, and callers accept any value >=0. Integer-water tests report settling while still oscillating.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#288 finding.open finding:LEVEL_WATER_FIX_POND_SPLITS_ARMS v0.1.0: level_water.surface_gaps records a touched pond's surface but never walks through it to its other wet mouths, so canal arms joined by a pond are scored as separate bodies and the surface gap is understated.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#289 trap.noted trap:the-python-reference-can-report-a-gap-under v0.1.0: The Python reference can report a gap under the 1 cm bar when the connected body violates it: arms at -0.2575 m and -0.2425 m around a pond at -0.2500 m report 0.0075 m, the true span being 0.015 m. Equivalence with the production diagnostic is not assumed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#290 finding.open finding:LIQUID_CONVERTER_FIX_PICKS_CRUDE_FIRST v0.1.0: CompLiquidConverter.StepFor returns the first eligible conversion, and the registry lists Crude distill before Industrial desalinate (and the toxic water rows alike), so the industrial recipes never run on machines that list both.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#291 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LIQUID_CONVERTER_FIX_PICKS_CRUDE_FIRST v0.1.0: Conversions were reordered highest tier first, so the industrial recipe is picked before the crude one for salt water and toxic water. (recorded on pre-landing bd36286c4795; landed as 015b7892e833)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#292 trap.noted trap:the-live-yield-check-a1-l1-is-not v0.1.0: The live yield check (A1, L1) is not recorded as run; the state is built only.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#293 finding.open finding:LIQUID_LEDGER_BUILD_NO_UNIT_LOST v0.1.0: Build FlowWorks liquid networks L1-L11 per liquid_network_semantics_design rev 2 §6: one transfer service with conservation fixes, components per topology revision, fill/draw order, tank jobs, hose free-end roles, pump as world mover, held volume and spill, liquid theft.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#294 finding.verified finding:LIQUID_LEDGER_BUILD_NO_UNIT_LOST v0.1.0: Built L1-L11 offline; live L1, Dump drain and pump pour, L5 spill and colonists cleaning, L4 ship tank fill/empty (78901 units each way, conserved) passed.; verified: steve-targeted (recorded on pre-landing a341da28fa98; landed as 97ef816aed51)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#295 cause.refuted cause:l5-colonists-clean-spill-unmeasured-on-run-20261018t233414 v0.1.0: L5_colonists_clean_spill UNMEASURED on run 20261018T233414 was a harness fault, not the mod: the precondition demanded Cleaning priority readBack 1 while vanilla stores 3 with manual priorities off; filth went 9 -> 0 in 750 ticks.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#296 cause.refuted cause:the-l5-run-1-miss-was-not-a v0.1.0: The L5 run 1 miss was not a mod fault: its 300-tick wait was shorter than the 375-tick flow lease.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#297 trap.noted trap:water-spills-as-a-surface-fill-not-filth v0.1.0: Water spills as a surface fill, not filth, so L5 water runs do not exercise the clean half. The 20261022 L5 FAIL could not tell water-home from loss until PlacedInWaterUnits/PlacedNearbyUnits counters were added (L5 SITE when drained to water, MOD FAIL on any uncounted unit).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#298 trap.noted trap:l4-ship-tank-passes-used-a-stand-in v0.1.0: L4 ship tank passes used a stand-in RM_UniversalCargoTank on a quicktest map, not a real gravship (L4_on_a_real_gravship has no fixture, SITE).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#299 trap.noted trap:l6-goodwill-needs-theft-day-to-measure-other v0.1.0: L6 goodwill needs --theft-day to measure; other L2/L4/L6 rows went UNMEASURED on a GovernorStop for hurt colonists, not on a mod fault.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#300 trap.noted trap:a9-owner-judges-spill-art-and-fitting-relabel v0.1.0: A9 (owner judges spill art and "Fitting:" relabel) is owner-only and unmeasured by any run.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#301 finding.open finding:LIQUID_PUMP_REDRAW_PLAN_VIEW_HUB v0.1.0: RM_LiquidPump and every PumpLook_* draw the pump as a face-on plan-view medallion beside oblique tanks and works, so it reads as a flat disc on the ground.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#302 trap.noted trap:the-modern-look-s-red-gloss-is-a v0.1.0: The Modern look's red gloss is a separate defect (MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY); this item covers only the camera angle.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#303 finding.open finding:MODCHECK_SUITE_CORRECTIONS_1 v0.1.0: First live modcheck wave (2026-09-13, min16): 2 GREEN, 12 RED, 2 aborted; most REDs are wrong suite assumptions, not mod defects.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#304 trap.noted trap:antiquities-wait-ticks-95000-and-shipmemory-wait-ticks v0.1.0: Antiquities wait_ticks(95000) and ShipMemory wait_ticks(700) failures were not mod faults: rimworld/step_game_ticks silently truncates (600-2800 per call) and the helper trusted it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#305 trap.noted trap:rimproperty-abort-and-half-of-aftermath-s-0 v0.1.0: RimProperty abort and half of Aftermath's 0/3 were not mod faults: a Pirate FactionDef with requiredCountAtGameStart>0 does not guarantee a live Faction, since Biotech's PirateWaster pre-empts it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#306 trap.noted trap:aftermath-s-harmony-patches-registered-failed-because-jawa v0.1.0: Aftermath's harmony_patches_registered failed because jawa/harmony_patches has no harmonyId parameter (only typeName, methodName).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#307 trap.noted trap:run-log-transient-modcheck-wave-bridge-20260913-log v0.1.0: Run log Transient/modcheck_wave_bridge_20260913.log is gone from disk; last copy is in git at 9da13212e8.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#308 trap.noted trap:no-live-re-run-has-happened-since-2026 v0.1.0: No live re-run has happened since 2026-09-13; fixes were verified by static analysis/git only, so the verify step is unmet. Other large-wait_ticks REDs may share the wait_ticks cause; re-check before fixing separately.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#309 finding.open finding:MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY v0.1.0: Modern FlowWorks pump, sprinkler, containers, hose ends and joins are red repaints of the Jawa/Industrial shapes, not real modern products.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:21Z BACKFILL#310 trap.noted trap:modern-containers-blue-drum-soda-bottle-blue-bucket v0.1.0: Modern containers (blue drum, soda bottle, blue bucket) and hose ends (chrome) are not red repaints; left alone.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#311 trap.noted trap:modern-joins-are-drawn-in-the-modern-pipe v0.1.0: Modern Joins are drawn in the Modern PIPE atlas red; a grey-PVC join or the sprinkler candidates' grey/black saddle foot on a red pipe would mismatch. Redraw joins only together with the Modern pipe atlas.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#312 trap.noted trap:drafted-pump-sprinkler-variants-are-registered-in-the v0.1.0: Drafted pump/sprinkler variants are registered in the art ledger but NOT installed, since they replace kept art.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#313 finding.open finding:MODERN_RED_LEFTOVERS_REDRAW_TODAYS_LOOK v0.1.0: 21 of 169 Modern PNGs under FlowWorks Textures are 10%+ bright red: 13 SuspensionBridge, 4 Hose/Styles/Modern reels, 2 Rivers/Modern bank stakes, RM_Bottle_m and RM_Bucket_m.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#314 trap.noted trap:rm-bottle-m-and-rm-bucket-m-are v0.1.0: RM_Bottle_m and RM_Bucket_m are also listed on MODERN_PUMP_REDRAW_SAME_RED_DIFFERENT_DAY; do not draw twice. The pump itself belongs to that item.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#315 finding.open finding:NATURAL_HEAT_CACHE_PROVE_STALE_DAYS v0.1.0: The natural-heat cache trusts terrain for 15,000 ticks after it changes (GPT G3.9, hook unverified), so room warming can continue after a hot pool is drained.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#316 trap.noted trap:sha-12c563a31-for-the-call-site-coverage-commit v0.1.0: Sha 12c563a31 for the call-site coverage commit does not resolve on origin/main; the commit there is 7e60caa7c (pre-rebase sha).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#317 finding.open finding:NATURAL_LIQUID_FIX_PUMPS_AS_FRESH_WATER v0.1.0: Natural liquids without canalFluid (e.g. RM_Liquid_ToxicWater, salt, brine, acid, coloured waters) get RM_Fluid_Water, so a pump on a toxic pond yields fresh water and canal discharge loses the liquid's identity.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#318 finding.claimed_fixed finding:NATURAL_LIQUID_FIX_PUMPS_AS_FRESH_WATER v0.1.0: Toxic water only now has its own pump yield and poison ladder (bd36286c4795 (pre-landing; landed as 015b7892e833)).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#319 finding.open finding:NORTH_STAR_WALK_AUTHORING_1 v0.1.0: 72 validation walks carry no '## north star' section; 25 of their subject: paths name vanished folders and 3 pairs of walks describe the same PNG.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#320 trap.noted trap:do-not-edit-the-north-star-sections-of v0.1.0: Do not edit the north star sections of Pits, Graffiti, WreckedMachines or FlowWorks: the recorded hash covers the whole section including prose, so any edit silently reverts the owner's validation. Edits that remove or relax a line in a validated section stay his.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#321 trap.noted trap:validating-a-north-star-creates-a-refusal-flowworks v0.1.0: Validating a north star creates a refusal: FlowWorks ships zero textures, so validating its 16 bars made it fail the visual floor.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#322 trap.noted trap:drafting-is-done-91-walks-653d9c7d3a-do-not v0.1.0: Drafting is done (91 walks, 653d9c7d3a); do not redraft. The original item text said 77 walks; the unauthored count is 72.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#323 finding.open finding:OUTFLOW_REGRESSION_FIX_HARDCODED_CAP v0.1.0: fw_pond_outflow_regression.py:14 hardcodes CAP=30, but capacity is bed levels x volumePerTile (shallow bed = 2 notches), so 'drained' is inflated: an undrained pond can report drained=18 and pass. Channel fills are never asserted.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#324 trap.noted trap:the-regression-passes-with-no-drainage-six-shallow v0.1.0: The regression passes with no drainage: six shallow cells hold 12 notch units, not the assumed 30, so an untouched pond reads drained=18 (> 6.5 threshold). If the inlet stays a source, every scored condition passes while the channel receives nothing.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#325 finding.open finding:P5_SITE_FIX_RERUN_DIGS_DEEPER v0.1.0: depth_rebuild_proofs P5 prepare_site repaints terrain but does not reset excavation; dig() adds deepenLevels to the existing cut, so a second --only p5 run on the same map measures deeper geometry.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#326 trap.noted trap:a-rerun-of-only-p5-on-the-same v0.1.0: A rerun of --only p5 on the same map can falsely fail the 152,000 L expectation because it measures deeper or capped geometry, not a mod fault; the default CLI permits the rerun.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#327 finding.open finding:PIT_RAIN_FIX_FRACTIONAL_HEADROOM_FULL v0.1.0: Rain uses whole-notch RoomNotches; a part-filled cell with less than one notch of headroom returns 0, so all rain goes to surface overflow instead of filling the excavation.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#328 finding.open finding:PIT_RAMP_FIX_WALKER_TAKES_FALL v0.1.0: A captured hostile walking down a valid ramp (joins up to MaxSpan 3) takes fall damage: IsFallingArrival judges by NaturalClimbLimitOf with no ramp exemption, and DescentFalls exempts only ladders/gear.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#329 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_RAMP_FIX_WALKER_TAKES_FALL v0.1.0: A walked step down a ramp is no longer classed as a fall, fixing the missing ramp exemption in the fall classification. (recorded on pre-landing e3b0a513a649; landed as 8c8bd865f358)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#330 finding.open finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_COUNTS_DOCTOR_OFF v0.1.0: AnyRescuerReaches counts colonists with Doctor unchecked in the Work tab as rescuers: WorkTypeIsDisabled tests capability, not the work assignment, so they suppress the rescue ladder indefinitely.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#331 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_COUNTS_DOCTOR_OFF v0.1.0: Only a colonist assigned Doctor now counts as a rescuer, so an unticked Doctor no longer suppresses the rescue ladder. (recorded on pre-landing e3b0a513a649; landed as 8c8bd865f358)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#332 finding.open finding:PIT_RESCUE_FIX_UNREACHABLE_SPARE_LADDER v0.1.0: SparePortable has no reachability check, so an unreachable minified ladder (e.g. in a sealed chamber) is chosen over building one; its install blueprint can never complete and later attempts decline to build another, stalling rescue.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#333 finding.open finding:PIT_SHOT_PROOF_FIX_OTHER_TARGET_PASSES v0.1.0: P7_shoot_between_7m_pits_refused passes whenever the picked target differs from eb, so if eb becomes a legal target but the AI picks another hostile, the row still passes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#334 trap.noted trap:p7-shoot-between-7m-pits-refused-can-be v0.1.0: P7_shoot_between_7m_pits_refused can be green for the wrong reason: the successful control shows target selection works, not why eb lost. Another hostile (e.g. the earlier trench-control target) being picked also passes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#335 finding.open finding:PIT_TRAVEL_SELFTEST_FIX_DOUBLE_COUNT v0.1.0: In PitTravel_every_trap_mask_reach_and_fall_read_asks_it, two regexes (same-line and \s*) both match both arrival detectors, giving 4 matches. The >=2 assertion still passes with the free-traveller guard removed from one detector.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:22Z BACKFILL#336 finding.verified finding:PIT_TRAVEL_SELFTEST_FIX_DOUBLE_COUNT v0.1.0: The summed same-line and \s* patterns became one \s* pattern counted once, so a dropped detector now reads 1 < 2 (PitAccessChecks.cs:147-149 against RM_SuperdeepTrap.cs:695,781).; verified: steve-targeted
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#337 trap.noted trap:the-record-s-testq-runs-on-stevedesktop-for v0.1.0: The record's testq runs on SteveDesktop for this item were INVALID or INTERRUPTED with no verdict, and several were quarantined for the Steve SIMD fault window; they must not be counted as passes. Only the [verify] pass rows count.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#338 trap.noted trap:the-ticket-s-original-line-numbers-pitaccesschecks-cs v0.1.0: The ticket's original line numbers (PitAccessChecks.cs:141-143, RM_SuperdeepTrap.cs:682,765-766) are stale after the fix; the fixed code is at PitAccessChecks.cs:147-149 and RM_SuperdeepTrap.cs:695,781.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#339 finding.open finding:PLACER_SELFCHECK_FIX_LEAVES_MERGED_ORE v0.1.0: RM_PlacerProof.SelfCheck cleanup tracks only newly created Thing ids. Generated ore that merged into an existing stack survives, so repeated selfchecks change colony resources and contaminate later live-state measurements.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#340 finding.open finding:POCKET_TRAP_FIX_LADDER_TO_SEALED_POCKET v0.1.0: RM_PocketGrid.TrappedIn passes a one-hop UsableLadderIn to Trapped. A ladder into another sealed pocket reads as an escape, so a stranded upright colonist returns not-trapped and gets no TryQueueFor rescue assistance.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#341 trap.noted trap:the-ticket-title-and-verdict-say-minor-but v0.1.0: The ticket title and verdict say MINOR, but the verbatim GPT finding is labelled severity MAJOR.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#342 finding.open finding:RAIDERS_TRENCH_BUILD_BREACH_AND_JUMP v0.1.0: Raiders facing an uncrossable trench stalled. When no colonist is reachable and the target is on another climb-band component, they must jump, scale ladders, or breach by shovelling a causeway (melee only, pit at or under the 4-square fill line); sieges are unchanged.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#343 trap.noted trap:the-latest-verify-is-a-fail-not-a v0.1.0: The latest verify is a FAIL, not a pass: bridge run 1 driver 7 row 5.2 RAID_FILL_CAUSEWAY, with no fill, no causeway, and the raid did not cross (measured MOD bar). The ticket is 'validated' despite the earlier passes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#344 trap.noted trap:known-limit-surface-to-surface-across-a-trench v0.1.0: Known limit: surface-to-surface across a trench is never one reachability hop (RelativeVeto), so after ladders stand the raider is sent into the trench and vanilla takes over. Colonists crossing by two ladders hit the same veto.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#345 trap.noted trap:the-a3-text-in-the-implemented-event-4 v0.1.0: The A3 text in the implemented event (4 m trench past 3 m portable reach is breached by fill) contradicts the spec's A3: a 4 m trench is NOT filled; only 2 m at the fill line is.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#346 trap.noted trap:the-10-jump-pack-share-for-raids-on v0.1.0: The 10% jump-pack share for raids on maps with 20+ dug squares is PROVISIONAL.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#347 finding.open finding:RAID_PLANK_FIX_SURFACE_HEIGHT_SUNKEN v0.1.0: RaidPlankFits accepts equal-height banks at any depth, but the raid plank record never sets deckNotches (left zero), so a plank between sunken banks is registered as a surface deck. Access, draw height, underpass class and fall distance come out wrong.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#348 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RAID_PLANK_FIX_SURFACE_HEIGHT_SUNKEN v0.1.0: Fixed in source (e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358)) so a raid plank between sunken banks lies at their depth; selftest went red then green. Live check still owed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#349 finding.open finding:REFUSED_HANDOVER_DRAIN_FIX_PUDDLE_OVERSTAYS v0.1.0: When a wet square becomes a Receiver/Sink, HandOverTakenCells credits it to the engine; what the channel refuses (full, or another liquid) stays on the square. On a dug/lake cell evaporation and soak are 0 and kernel flow skips non-HoldsSheet kinds, so the residue may never leave. Unverified live.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#350 finding.open finding:REVIEW_MAP_FIX_STATIONS_NO_LEAD_OUT v0.1.0: In review_map.py, hose lays at stations 63, 66 and 67 are refused ('no room for the straight lead-out from the nozzle', layOk false), so those stations show no hose. At stations 35/36 'Ladder' never reads 'Ladder up'.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#351 finding.open finding:REVIEW_STAGE_FIX_SMALL_MAPS_HELD_GATES v0.1.0: owner_review_stage.py places bars_* rows off-map on a 250 map (bars site at origin+150) without checking map size. Held-open gates read open=False after gate_open though by hand the mod holds them open. The bars_trench shooter has no ranged verb.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#352 trap.noted trap:mod-fault-in-held-open-gates-by-hand v0.1.0: Mod fault in held-open gates: by hand on the same map, Hold open plus a colonist ordered in opens the sluice (open=True seals=False) and it stays open after the colonist leaves; the failure is in the stage's walk (water_scenes_walk.gate_open).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#353 trap.noted trap:the-stage-s-label-truncates-gate-open-s v0.1.0: The stage's label truncates gate_open's report to 60 chars, hiding its [button pressed|NOT PRESSED] / [walked|NO COLONIST] detail.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#354 trap.noted trap:the-wetted-ground-state-does-not-need-building v0.1.0: The wetted-ground state does not need building in 0.1.0: the owner moved the wetting bars to 0.2.0 with the thin film.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#355 trap.noted trap:the-five-red-offline-tests-wetting-wetness-probe v0.1.0: The five red offline tests (wetting, wetness probe, toss flame, fetch fallen, coating) are red as intended because the behaviour does not exist yet; red there is not a regression.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#356 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_BREAK_PIT_COVER_UNDER_THROWN_STACK_PAWNS_ONLY v0.1.0: ExplosiveKnockback scene sc_storeroom_bomb_pit expects a thrown stack on a covered pit cell to fall through with one descent and destroy the cover, but no code does it. Building_SpanDeck's load counts living walking pawns only, and RM_ThingFlyer_Knockback never touches a cover.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#357 trap.noted trap:the-scene-text-is-correct-and-the-code v0.1.0: The scene text is correct and the code is what is missing. The locked, certified leaf thrown_into_pit_falls and sheet.md:44 say a thrown good onto a covered cell falls through and breaks the cover. Do not correct the scene text.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#358 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_BURY_GSS_CORDS_IN_FW_SUPERDEEP_PIT_WATER v0.1.0: Gimme Some Slack draws a waterproof conduit across FlowWorks' RM_Fill_Water_Superdeep (2 m+) as one cord lying on the water, battery to consumer. The same crossing over vanilla WaterDeep dives with two water stubs at the banks. Deep swim on or off makes no difference.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#359 trap.noted trap:not-the-deep-swim-bug-the-superdeep-cell v0.1.0: Not the deep-swim bug: the Superdeep cell is path-walkable with swim on and off, so GSS has always drawn a cord there.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#360 trap.noted trap:snapshotblock-of-buries-only-water-whose-terraindef-is v0.1.0: SnapshotBlock.Of buries only water whose TerrainDef is Impassable. RM_Fill_Water_Superdeep inherits WaterShallow (Standable, WaterproofConduitable), unlike vanilla WaterDeep, so GSS cannot tell it is deep. FlowWorks decides deep in RM_Swim.IsDeepSwimIndex, which GSS cannot see.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#361 trap.noted trap:brim-1-5-m-and-vanilla-watermovingchestdeep-also v0.1.0: Brim (1.5 m) and vanilla WaterMovingChestDeep also draw a cord (stubs 0, cord_edges 1), consistent with GSS's 'walkable = ground' rule. They are meant to stay wadeable ground.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#362 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_CHECK_SHOOTING_RULE_OFF_PICKS_FARTHER_PIT_FOE v0.1.0: With the superdeep shooting rule OFF, the shooter picks a farther target (11098) than the nearer pit enemy (Human11094). Chain toggle_superdeep_shooting row shooting_rule_off FAILs; shooting_rule_on_pit_only reads UNMEASURED.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:23Z BACKFILL#363 trap.noted trap:it-is-not-known-whether-this-is-a v0.1.0: It is not known whether this is a mod fault (OFF does not restore vanilla targeting) or a harness fault (vanilla target choice is not strictly nearest, so the row's expectation is wrong). Do not assume either.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#364 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_DESIGN_FLOWWORKS_DIG_INTERFACE_CONSOLIDATION v0.1.0: The FlowWorks dig interface is a daunting grid of options (5 Orders designators, a 40-entry 'Dig to depth' menu, 11 Structure special designators, 33 Architect buildables) and unlike the game's usual style; dig speed is slow. Needs one consolidated UI for dig, repair, fill in and the rest.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#365 trap.noted trap:the-depth-menu-s-digger-days-estimate-omits v0.1.0: The depth menu's digger-days estimate omits the x4 labour multiplier, so it understates the time. Defaults: first soil half-metre 12800 work, about 7529 ticks (3.0 h) at MiningSpeed 1.0; rock 12 h.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#366 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_DRYRUN_SCENES_ON_EMPTY_GAME_THREE_PASSED v0.1.0: In-game scene rows can read PASS with nothing on the map. With every bridge call answering a bare success, three rows pass on 99ff70d91a (scene_fetch_fallen; PTR_control_open_pit_no_one_falls; SRC_flow_stops_when_empty) and SRC_flow_stops_when_empty still passes on ed7e3b5389.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#367 trap.noted trap:the-one-clause-passing-at-ed7e3b5389-fail-1 v0.1.0: The one clause passing at ed7e3b5389 (FAIL 1/8) passes only because it reads the old scene file from git; it does not exercise the new module, which does not exist yet.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#368 trap.noted trap:scenes-of-other-mods-modcheck-suite-scenes-of v0.1.0: Scenes of other mods (modcheck Suite scenes of Graffiti and Pyrelands) use a different harness; the clause covers only the FlowWorks scene harness, so they read 'did not run', not pass.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#369 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_EARN_CALIBRATION_BOTH_WAYS v0.1.0: A covered verdict signs under an UNTESTED brief (calibration read only on not_covered). calibrate promotes on free_check exit 0 and always writes the registry (--no-run too). Submit raises repair_due and alarms before reading calibration.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#370 trap.noted trap:calibrate-passing-on-free-check-exit-0-does v0.1.0: calibrate passing on free_check exit 0 does not show the planted cases ran.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#371 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIT_SENSE_V3_WRAPUP_NEVER_FINISHED_IN_FIFTEEN v0.1.0: After the rm-metronome.timer cutover, sense families v3 and wrapup never finish inside the 15 s limit, even when retried alone, so they go blind. 23 of 45 beats overran versus 161 of 1269 before.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#372 trap.noted trap:load-average-9-on-14-cores-with-5 v0.1.0: Load average ~9 on 14 cores with 5 detached Opus helpers and a FlowWorks selftest suite was running; the record does not establish load as the cause.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#373 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_JAWABENCH_AWARENESS_INSTALL_CRASHES_START v0.1.0: SteveDesktop game A crashes natively at startup on the first `up` after `steve.sh deploy` (3 of 3 in one sitting); a restart came up clean (4 of 4). Stacks run through JawaBench awareness/TPS Harmony install from bridge provider construction.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#374 trap.noted trap:the-jawabench-awareness-frame-is-not-the-only v0.1.0: The JawaBench awareness frame is not the only crash site: the third crash came right after '[FlowWorks] hose essential patches', before the Harmony count line, and one was a mid-run crash with no managed frame.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#375 trap.noted trap:whether-the-post-deploy-first-launch-matters-is v0.1.0: Whether the post-deploy first launch matters is unproven; the restart also does a `down` first.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#376 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FIX_STRANDED_COLONIST_READS_UNTRAPPED_NO_RESCUE v0.1.0: A colonist inside a roofed, sealed 5x5 pit dug 8 notches (4 m) reads held=False trapped=False though no move leaves the pocket (drafted order moved 0 cells; he wanders and sleeps there). The stranded-colonist rescue never queues.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#377 trap.noted trap:trapped-false-for-an-own-faction-colonist-does v0.1.0: trapped=False for an own-faction colonist does not mean not stranded: 'not held' is read as 'not stranded' when the pocket has no climbable exit.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#378 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_FOLD_FRAME_JUDGE_INTO_PRELOOK v0.1.0: Two judges exist: the north-star frame judge (judge_cli.py) and the pre-look scene judge (scene_look_judge_brief.md). No FlowWorks bar has a frame judge (inventory row 114, UNMEASURED).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#379 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_JUDGE_FLOWWORKS_REVIEW_SAVE_FOUR_LOOK_QUESTIONS v0.1.0: Review save shows looks no bar decides: dry 1-notch canal reads as a low wall; 5 m water bluer than 2 m; colonists in 0.5 m and 8 m dry holes draw alike; bleed-well hose end seems short of tap; geyser pit suffix says Water for tar/oil/blood; owner_look_regressions 130+7 FAIL.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#380 trap.noted trap:item-3-equal-draw-at-0-5-m v0.1.0: Item 3 (equal draw at 0.5 m and 8 m) conflicts with state reads that say the draw offset follows depth (plot_H_depth_ladder PASS); the PASS does not settle how it looks at this zoom.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#381 trap.noted trap:owner-look-regressions-fails-130-ol-no-surface v0.1.0: owner_look_regressions FAILs (130 OL_no_surface_shadow, 7 OL_labels_fade) come from a judge that predates the shadow-under-the-sheet design, so they are not a mod regression by themselves.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:24Z BACKFILL#382 trap.noted trap:the-two-concealed-pit-stations-54-63-had v0.1.0: The two concealed-pit stations (54, 63) had one plank lid on the centre of a 3x3 pit, so the pit showed plainly; owner_review_stage.py now lids every square, so earlier captures of them mislead.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#383 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_LAND_RALPH_FLOWWORKS_PIT_FEATURES v0.1.0: The Ralph FlowWorks branch built pit features that never reached main: raider rescue, bedless pit prison, hung bridge, ladder rigs, one depth drawing, sod on covers, pit looks, raid-stall fixes, rain fill rate. Port the ones the 0.1.0 design asks for.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#384 trap.noted trap:the-branch-s-sink-numbers-0-37-1 v0.1.0: The branch's sink numbers (0.37..1.50 at 0.5..4 m, knee curve, pitSinkPerLevel 0.3) contradict the owner-confirmed sink of 0.05 per half metre on main (23151d18be). Take the drawing, never the numbers.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#385 trap.noted trap:the-clone-unlanded-alarm-is-not-lost-work v0.1.0: The clone_unlanded alarm is not lost work: all 36 commits are on origin/ralph/flowworks (tip 69c6028177); the checker counts them unlanded because they are on no main-only mirror tip.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#386 trap.noted trap:the-branch-s-dig-progress-is-superseded-by v0.1.0: The branch's dig progress is superseded by main d23e9b6a83: do not port it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#387 trap.noted trap:the-branch-s-last-note-recorded-selftest-1282 v0.1.0: The branch's last note recorded selftest 1282/1285 with three reds it called pre-existing, so a green branch selftest is not assured.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#388 trap.noted trap:the-off-control-and-the-corrosion-rows-passed v0.1.0: The OFF control and the corrosion rows passed in the same run, so the failure is only in the ignition-ON row.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#389 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_PROVE_FLOWWORKS_TOGGLES_DENSE v0.1.0: Dense live proofs of four FlowWorks toggles (fallDamageEnabled, liquidCorrosionEnabled, liquidIgnitionEnabled, superdeepShootingRuleEnabled), ladder_required_off, and a re-run of six standing FAIL/UNMEASURED S3 chains were owed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#390 trap.noted trap:ingest-skips-exact-tier-proofs-relentless-game-reads v0.1.0: Ingest skips exact-tier proofs (relentless_game reads them), so no verify event was recorded for the runs; the results live in the Steve logs, not the ticket.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#391 trap.noted trap:the-3-jobs-before-fd568d495b-died-rmdatarootunset-because v0.1.0: The 3 jobs before fd568d495b died RmDataRootUnset because the proof lines did not keep RM_DATA_ROOT in WSLENV; those are not mod results.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#392 trap.noted trap:selftest-flowworks-northstar-tier-want-rows-are-red v0.1.0: selftest_flowworks_northstar tier-want rows are red since 285a403024 added mandrake.rm.patches to the flowworks/gss tiers; that red is pre-existing and not from this ticket.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#393 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_RESUME_SWEPT_PAWN_STUCK_MID_RIVER v0.1.0: RM_MapComponent_RiverCurrent.Move() calls pather.StopDead() + Notify_Teleported(false) without ending the job, so a Goto toil waits forever; a shoved pawn rides the current to the next bend or edge and stands frozen until drafted.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#394 trap.noted trap:flowworks-selftest-is-verse-free-so-it-cannot v0.1.0: FlowWorks' selftest is Verse-free, so it cannot reproduce this; a green offline suite says nothing about it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#395 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_SUBMERGE_PIT_FITTINGS_BY_LEVEL v0.1.0: Pit liquid is a base-terrain swap, so ladders, the ramp, spikes, gates, items and swale drew whole over the liquid at every depth. Owner's live look found wall ladders, portable ladders, ramp, items and tar still wrong, and D8 inconsistent; shadows ringed the full D8 pit.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#396 trap.noted trap:the-wall-face-is-drawn-from-the-fill v0.1.0: The wall face is drawn from the fill grid, which can lag the true level (grid 3 vs 4.00 at cell 113,104), so using the true level for the ladder cut hid it under the wall band. That fix (HungLadderLevel) was in the since-deleted RM_SubmergeMath; 441c321a20dc now uses the true level for wall faces too.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#397 trap.noted trap:swale-and-goods-under-liquid-were-not-covered v0.1.0: Swale and goods under liquid were not covered by the first build; the owner is unsure of the expected swale behavior, and the decision is filed separately.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#398 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_SWEEP_WATER_SOLVERS_ON_BIG_MAPS v0.1.0: A Python bakeoff must choose FlowWorks's water solver at 250/325/400 maps (river, lake, ocean edge, dam break, pit fill, swell), with cost projected to C#. It must name a winner and runner-up with a cost table and score every plan 5.4 pass bar per arm, frames judged blind.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#399 trap.noted trap:the-criss-cross-reservoir-ripples-were-mostly-the v0.1.0: The criss-cross reservoir ripples were mostly the engine: refuted, the compare pages' clock (full steps plus a short tail step) caused most of it. Roughness at 33 s was 0.18 m with ragged steps vs 0.0003 m with even steps. Fixed in compare.py (a72cc7f).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#400 trap.noted trap:explicit-dampers-inside-the-theta-alpha-stability-margin v0.1.0: Explicit dampers inside the theta/alpha stability margin do not cure the ripples: they cut reservoir roughness only 10-17%, failing the 1.3x bar, and stronger ones blow the lake at rest. Seven of eight dampers fail.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#401 trap.noted trap:the-froude-cap-does-not-fix-the-reservoir v0.1.0: The Froude cap does not fix the reservoir ripples; the owner was told so plainly and ripple fixes are next-version work.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#402 trap.noted trap:the-old-pit-dig-scene-measured-a-dry v0.1.0: The old pit_dig scene measured a dry pit for every row: the canal was cut a fixed 2 m into a bank rising away from the river, leaving a ridge. Earlier pit-dig rows are invalid. Explicit diffusion and conjugate-gradient implicit are too slow to run in the test, so their pit fill is untested.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:25Z BACKFILL#403 trap.noted trap:the-old-moat-lost-only-0-24-m v0.1.0: The old moat lost only 0.24 m of 1.5 m and barely paled, so it did not show drying; it now has a tilted floor and a deliberately exaggerated evaporation rate.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#404 trap.noted trap:the-twin-grid-is-250-x-40-not v0.1.0: The twin grid is 250 x 40, not the 100 x 100 the rivers spec says.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#405 finding.open finding:RIMMASTER_MOVE_IMAGE_SKILLS_OUT_PAST_CODE_PATHS v0.1.0: General skills generating-images and editing-images still live in this repo: 32 tracked code files and 39 docs name skills/generating-images/... paths, and the running art pipeline calls their scripts, so they cannot yet move to MandrakeWorkflows.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#406 trap.noted trap:removing-the-skills-folder-breaks-every-art-job v0.1.0: Removing the skills folder breaks every art job until rm-artpiped restarts: it computes common.DEFAULT_WORKER_SCRIPT (and the Gemini worker) once at import. codex_image.py imports rmpaths from parents[3]/src/RimMandrake/Utils and does not run outside this repo.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#407 finding.open finding:RIVER_COLORS_WIDEN_RED_BLUE_GREEN_DEATH v0.1.0: FlowWorks river colour is a per-tile biome switch (RiverTint.ColorFor): four RM biomes get settings colours, all else waterColor. No per-tile override and no source ramp red -> bluish -> thick green -> fade.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#408 trap.noted trap:steps-are-hex-sized-since-one-tile-draws v0.1.0: Steps are hex-sized since one tile draws one colour; a smooth ramp inside a tile needs a vertex-recolouring postfix. 'Death' is visual only (alpha/width fade); ending a river is a link edit, not colour.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#409 finding.open finding:RIVER_TOGGLE_PROOFS_BUILD_ELEVEN_UNCLAIMED v0.1.0: The 11 river settings toggles and the untested river-walk lines owed in validation.py lack live proof; flier/flood lines need the owner watching.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#410 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIVER_TOGGLE_PROOFS_BUILD_ELEVEN_UNCLAIMED v0.1.0: A5 was stale: the weir review 2026-10-23 removed weir drift rolls (owner: No auto fish capture); criterion now names the capture + flotsam proofs (recorded on pre-landing 82a1caaab17c; landed as 0eff53e3a4f4)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#411 trap.noted trap:offline-l0-criteria-o1-o2-are-done-riverswitchflip v0.1.0: Offline L0 criteria O1/O2 are done (RiverSwitchFlip_<toggle> green, RIVER_TOGGLES_OWED empty), but no toggle row ran in 20261012g/13b ext_river (only 5 chains). Old A5 (weir drift rolls) was stale; weir drift removed, now ProofWeirCapture and ProofRiverFlotsam.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#412 finding.open finding:SELFTEST_ASSERT_CLOSE_FIX_ACCEPTS_NAN v0.1.0: AssertClose in SelfTest/Program.cs (~118-123) tests Math.Abs(got-want) > eps, which is false for NaN, so a NaN result passes every case relying on it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#413 trap.noted trap:any-selftest-numeric-assertion-through-assertclose-is-green v0.1.0: Any SelfTest numeric assertion through AssertClose is green on NaN unless another assertion rejects it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#414 finding.open finding:SESSION_HURT_DIAGNOSE_BRUISED_AT_THE_BELL v0.1.0: In FlowWorks live_session B3, colonists Elisa/Godders were hurt mid-script (Bruise, Crack, RM_SwimExhaustion), so GovernorStop voided climb_proof, sprinkler_live and liquid_checks L2/L4/L5/L6. Hurt bystanders recurred in later runs.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#415 finding.verified finding:SESSION_HURT_DIAGNOSE_BRUISED_AT_THE_BELL v0.1.0: Parking no longer walks bystanders across the river (the cause of the swimming bruises). They are teleported into a 5x5 pen read dry by get_terrain_batch, off all zones and plots, then drafted. Start colonists are fed RM_Thirst. A per-script hediff census was added.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#416 cause.refuted cause:spores-as-the-cause-of-the-bruise-crack v0.1.0: Spores as the cause of the Bruise/Crack on the parked, drafted pawn Vikinger: ruled out, the pawn was still hurt (ended 160,95 with Bruise + Crack) after the spore purge.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#417 cause.refuted cause:idle-or-drafted-colonists-left-on-cells-later v0.1.0: Idle or drafted colonists left on cells later scripts dig was the first hypothesis; the mechanism found was park_bystanders walking everyone across the river at z~94 on the fish map, so they were swimming and carried downstream.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#418 trap.noted trap:order-pawn-success-means-arrived-so-with-waitticks v0.1.0: order_pawn success means ARRIVED, so with waitTicks=1 every park read False; a parked reading did not prove the pawn was held.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#419 finding.open finding:SHOWCASE_RULES_TUNE_AWAITING_STAGE_THREE v0.1.0: src/RimMandrake/rimflow/showcase_rules.json holds two first-guess numbers (max_overlap 0.25, min_new_after_first 3), status UNMEASURED. They need calibrating on the first two concepts to reach Designer stage 3.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#420 trap.noted trap:the-numbers-look-like-settings-but-are-unmeasured v0.1.0: The numbers look like settings but are unmeasured first guesses. FlowWorks is at stage 2, so nothing can be calibrated yet.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#421 finding.open finding:SMALL_PUMP_HOSE_FIX_LAYS_UNCONNECTED v0.1.0: Targeting an isolated RM_LiquidPump with hose Deploy or Move lays the end on the pump's own cell. The order succeeds but the hose stays hydraulically disconnected. Severity MAJOR.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#422 trap.noted trap:the-hose-order-reports-success-even-though-no v0.1.0: The hose order reports success even though no coupling forms. RM_LiquidPump is 1x1 PassThroughOnly, so its cell is Walkable and ApproachIfOnPort returns early; SideOf(0,0) is -1.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#423 finding.open finding:SUNKEN_DRAWBRIDGE_FIX_REJECTS_HINGE_BANK v0.1.0: A sunken drawbridge's landing search starts at its hinge anchors, but `passable` rejects squares with ClimbDepthAt>0 unless decked. Every start is rejected, no bank search occurs, and riders fall as overflow.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#424 trap.noted trap:the-ticket-title-and-verdict-say-minor-but v0.1.0: The ticket title and verdict say MINOR but the quoted GPT finding says severity MAJOR; the verification verdict is CONFIRMED MINOR.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:26Z BACKFILL#425 finding.open finding:SUSPENSION_BASE_REWIRE_COVERS_STEP_OUT v0.1.0: Building_PitCover must be lifted into a span base class, with one read RM_SpanUtility.DeckedAt(map, c, out float deckHeightM) replacing IsCovered at seven call sites. It is a pure refactor with no behaviour change.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#426 trap.noted trap:the-2026-10-19-live-fail-120-kg v0.1.0: The 2026-10-19 live FAIL (120 kg held 121.0 kg, 220 kg held 244.3 kg) was not a mod defect: the cover rows called set_draft without drafted=True (a no-op), so colonists wandered off the deck. The mod read clean (LoadOn sums the whole deck, threshold is weakest rating x sensitivity, covers never floor-band).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#427 trap.noted trap:the-recorded-fail-verify-of-2026-10-19 v0.1.0: The recorded FAIL verify of 2026-10-19 (rerun_20261019T010244) is a harness artefact; disregard it. The harness was fixed in 5c921893e8 so rows draft and read back, and a pawn off its deck cell now reads UNMEASURED.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#428 trap.noted trap:a2-is-only-partial-code-review-status-check v0.1.0: A2 is only partial: code_review_status check on 2026-10-24 left three touched files DIRTY, needing a review pass and mark-clean: SelfTest/SuspensionBridgeChecks.cs, SelfTest/Program.cs, Pits/Suspension/RM_MapComponent_SuspensionBridges.cs.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#429 finding.open finding:SWALE_CANAL_ART_REFERENCE_1 v0.1.0: The RM_Swale art is a placeholder. It must be specced from screenshots of a real FlowWorks canal, and the placeholder note removed from the sheet manifest once the new art passes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#430 trap.noted trap:rm-swale-v2-brown-rippled-cracked-bank-strip v0.1.0: RM_Swale_v2 (brown, rippled, cracked-bank strip) was judged not to read like the canal beside it: live FlowWorks water is flat blue-grey with no bank art. v2 was not wired.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#431 finding.open finding:SWIM_WARNING_FIX_ANY_PAWN_MESSAGE v0.1.0: SWIM_armour_warned_before_under accepts any new swim/tired/drown/shore/exhaust message from any pawn, and warned<=under accepts the same sample. The drowning announcement therefore counts as advance warning, and the check passes with the early warning broken.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#432 trap.noted trap:swim-armour-warned-before-under-can-pass-with v0.1.0: SWIM_armour_warned_before_under can PASS with the early tiring warning broken: 'exhausted and going under' or 'is drowning' matches the filter in the same sample that first sees submersion, and equality is accepted.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#433 finding.open finding:TAKEN_BY_LAND_SERVICE_1 v0.1.0: The gale carry (RM_DuneGale) and FlowWorks' swept-away (RM_WorldComponent_SweptAway) are the same hold/letter/return mechanism built twice, and a pawn taken by the land leaves no ground trace at the spot.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#434 trap.noted trap:prooftaken-returning-unmeasured-no-taken-by-land-component v0.1.0: ProofTaken returning `UNMEASURED no taken-by-land component` means no world was loaded; it is never a pass. gale=True is required only if Stillsand is loaded, otherwise it is reported, not a failure.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#435 trap.noted trap:runner-jobs-quarantined-inside-steve-simd-fault-windows v0.1.0: Runner jobs quarantined inside Steve SIMD fault windows (e.g. J20261101T070105Z-adb7ec.a1, J20261103T235805Z-399647.a1, J20261104T000523Z-7c7de5.a1) must not be counted; their verdicts do not stand, though some steve-targeted pass events remain in the log.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#436 finding.open finding:TAR_COOLING_GLASS_MEND_ENGINE_CELLS v0.1.0: Tar cooling writes glass onto cells the depth engine owns (Flood_FlowWorks.cs ~377-390).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#437 trap.noted trap:the-call-site-coverage-commit-on-origin-main v0.1.0: The call-site coverage commit on origin/main is 7e60caa7c; the sha 12c563a31 was its pre-rebase sha and is not on main.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#438 finding.open finding:TAR_LAG_FIX_NO_WATER_CONTROL v0.1.0: The tar viscosity check asserts only 1<=wet<len(cells) with no water control, so a tar front moving at the ordinary one-cell-per-pulse rate also passes (six wet of ten cells after six pulses).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#439 trap.noted trap:tar-front-lags-water-does-not-compare-against v0.1.0: tar_front_lags_water does not compare against water; any partial front passes, so a green result does not show viscosity lag. The water-front check now expects gradual propagation.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#440 finding.open finding:TAR_REFINERY_FIX_CAN_FALLBACK_DEAD v0.1.0: Tar refineries' descriptions promise cans stacked beside the machine, but the item-output fallback never runs: the tar refine row has both product and productThing, and CompLiquidConverter uses tank output whenever product is non-null. With no chemfuel output tank the refinery stops.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#441 finding.claimed_fixed finding:TAR_REFINERY_FIX_CAN_FALLBACK_DEAD v0.1.0: Descriptions made true instead of adding a can-count balance call: the three tar refinery descriptions no longer promise cans, and the unreachable productThing was removed from the RM_Liquid_Tar refine row.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#442 trap.noted trap:code-doc-4a34f746d4-rules-that-items-are-never v0.1.0: Code doc 4a34f746d4 rules that items are never a fallback for a full tank; the item-output fallback only exists when product is null.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#443 finding.open finding:TESTQ_MODS_WIRE_BEYOND_FLOWWORKS v0.1.0: Only 1 of 132 harnessed mods is selectable by testq. The next mods must be wired by bridge backlog, each with a bad_selection probe feeding coverage measure L5.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:27Z BACKFILL#444 trap.noted trap:the-harnessed-mod-count-is-132-in-the v0.1.0: The harnessed-mod count is 132 in the title but 130 in the coverage output.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#445 finding.open finding:TIMBER_WINCH_FIX_POWERS_STEEL_BRIDGE v0.1.0: A timber winch placed beside a steel drawbridge hinge links by adjacency only with no tier match, and reports Powered without a power comp, so the steel leaf moves without electricity.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#446 finding.open finding:TRANSFER_AUDIT_FIX_FAILED_MOVE_COUNTS v0.1.0: The Transfer audit's CommittedBlocks regex `==\s*\w` also matches `== 0` (nothing committed), so Lint accepts a credit inside a failed-Move branch. Latent: no such site exists today.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#447 trap.noted trap:the-existing-negative-controls-only-test-credits-outside v0.1.0: The existing negative controls only test credits outside blocks, so they do not catch a credit inside a `== 0` branch.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#448 finding.open finding:UNDERPASS_SHOT_FIX_OVERRIDES_VANILLA_REJECT v0.1.0: The CanHitTargetFrom postfix replaces a false vanilla result with only the cliff test when both ends are floor-side, so a wall-blocked shot under a deck becomes legal and ProofShot and combat AI can accept it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#449 finding.claimed_fixed finding:UNDERPASS_SHOT_FIX_OVERRIDES_VANILLA_REJECT v0.1.0: Fixed in source at e3b0a513a649 (pre-landing; landed as 8c8bd865f358): the postfix reopens only a cliff-only rejection and re-asks vanilla, so a wall-blocked shot stays refused. Selftest was red then green; live check owed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#450 finding.open finding:VV_MATRIX_FIX_SURFACE_FAILURES_UNDERCOUNT v0.1.0: ShippedMatrixProbe Rates sets failures = max(a,b,c), a lower bound exported as the failure count. Two scenes failing settling and two different scenes failing conservation report 2 failures, not 4, understating rates.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#451 trap.noted trap:the-failures-field-reads-as-an-exact-count v0.1.0: The `failures` field reads as an exact count but is a lower bound; the per-check split is only in the example, and `examples` does not make the aggregate exact.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#452 finding.open finding:WALKS3_OUTFLOW_FIX_HOSE_WONT_JUMP_DITCH v0.1.0: water_scenes_walk s3_geom() sets outflow=canal[0] and _pump_rig lays hose B onto that dug depth-1 square. That is hose band 1, and CordWorld.StepAllowed refuses a band change without a ramp/ladder link, so the lay reads 'no route'.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#453 trap.noted trap:a-bank-end-sitting-gaps-proof-s-s3 v0.1.0: A bank end (sitting_gaps_proof's S3['outflow_bank']) may not be enough: S3.a/S3.b need the outflow to pour into the canal.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#454 finding.open finding:WEIR_CATCH_FIX_ESCAPES_CAP_RADIUS v0.1.0: GenPlace Near placement of a captured stack is unbounded, while HeldCatch counts only within HeldCatchRadius 2.5. A stack displaced beyond 2.5 cells is not counted, so further captures can exceed the held-item cap and a breach wash omits it.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:28Z BACKFILL#455 finding.open finding:BOTTLE_STOCK_MEND_FREE_REFILLS v0.1.0: Filling a bottle/bucket/barrel from pond terrain and washing a container never debit the body's stock, so a bottle poured into a tank creates liquid from a limited pond.
