- 2026-11-16T08:32:33Z BACKFILL#581 finding.open finding:JOINT_GATE_HARDEN_TRUST_THE_DRAWN_BOX v0.1.0: The FlowWorks joint gate did not measure the drawn collar box against each nozzle/port point, had no under-building or through-wall rows, no per-joint crop, and the walkthrough save did not refuse while the gate was red.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:33Z BACKFILL#582 trap.noted trap:the-recorded-sha-5547ca7a0967-was-replaced-by-the v0.1.0: The recorded sha 5547ca7a0967 was replaced by the 2026-10-29 history rewrite and resolves in no clone, so the arbiter's G1_sha FAIL is a gate defect, not a code fault. The arbiter left the ticket UNRESOLVED; G4_evidence also failed and stands until judged.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#583 trap.noted trap:map-0-fails-the-gate-while-the-staged v0.1.0: Map 0 fails the gate while the staged C7 pipe stub [25,130] stands. The green save half passed on a map with 0 joints, so it is not evidence of a hose joint on map 0.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#584 finding.open finding:LADDER_ROWS_WIDEN_STILL_COUNT_TO_TWO v0.1.0: S1_dig_ladder, X1_pawn_height_ladder (a Hare), plot H/B looks still stage the old notch ladder stopping at 2 m, while the depth bars are restated in metres (pit 8 m, pawn 4 m, six fill looks).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#585 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LADDER_ROWS_WIDEN_STILL_COUNT_TO_TWO v0.1.0: S1 and X1 widened to D 8/16 with a colonist, and plot H/B widened. (recorded on pre-landing 7f585adf7; landed as 47ddee0286b6)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#586 trap.noted trap:fill-under-25-cm-stays-unmeasured-because-there v0.1.0: Fill under 25 cm stays UNMEASURED because there is no sub-notch fill tool, so the fill-tier row cannot be read green for that rung.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#587 trap.noted trap:x1-is-known-red-on-the-sink-cap v0.1.0: X1 is known-red on the sink cap, so a red X1 does not by itself show a regression of the widening.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#588 finding.open finding:LIQUID_DEPTH_REWIRE_FIVE_IS_DEAD v0.1.0: FlowWorks code, defs and settings must follow the ruled depth spec §7.2: one ruler in metres and litres, FillTier by metres of water, one MaxDepth, one burn rate, the 5:1 label and sourceBudgetMultiplier deleted, and labels and mouseover in metres.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#589 trap.noted trap:the-title-is-partly-wrong-pit-stays-2 v0.1.0: The title is partly wrong: 'pit stays 2 m (D5)' was reversed by the owner's 2026-10-17 card (dig default 8 m, setting 2-20 m), and water no longer moves in whole notches. Build neither.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#590 trap.noted trap:the-floor-shape-per-lake-and-biome-roughness v0.1.0: The floor shape per lake and biome roughness moved to LAKE_FLOORS_BUILD_MOUNTAINS_RUN_DEEP; the fixed 20 m default and 50 m large-lake rule are gone. 50 m is only an upper bound for mountainous or deep biomes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#591 trap.noted trap:the-a2-verify-at-2026-10-24-was v0.1.0: The A2 verify at 2026-10-24 was refused on seat ownership, and the 2026-10-17 live check did not show settings UI or mouseover by eye. The A2 pass rests on the B2_readout_metres_litres row.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#592 finding.open finding:LIQUID_FIRE_MEND_COVER_AND_SAVE v0.1.0: RM_LiquidFire does not save carriedAcc/carriedTick/carriedFluid/burningFluid, so after load TakeCarried treats a null fluid as matching any. ApplyEffects ignores cover and Flying, setting pawns on an intact cover and flyers alight at the trapped 100% rate.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#593 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LIQUID_FIRE_MEND_COVER_AND_SAVE v0.1.0: Carried and burning fluids are now saved; flyers and cover are respected in ApplyEffects; blast hops are keyed by Explosion id instead of centre cell. (recorded on pre-landing 9bca729c18; landed as 86c093c8c5d1)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#594 trap.noted trap:closed-on-an-offline-selftest-only-gptfixesachecks-no v0.1.0: Closed on an offline selftest only (GptFixesAChecks); no live proof run, so the in-game behaviour of cover, flyers and save/load is unverified.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#595 finding.open finding:LIQUID_ROTATION_FIX_STARVED_STRANGER v0.1.0: Consumer rotation is keyed map-wide by LiquidDef, so a starved machine on another network stalls a supplied one forever (MayDraw, RM_LiquidNetKernel.cs:447). A multi-input converter always runs its first liquid because FirstRunnable returns the first positive.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#596 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LIQUID_ROTATION_FIX_STARVED_STRANGER v0.1.0: Rotation is now keyed per store with a forfeit after one yield (fixing the map-wide stall), and multi-input converters use NextRunnable so they rotate liquids. (recorded on pre-landing 69a9a10adb; landed as 7e492a762f01)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#597 trap.noted trap:closed-on-an-offline-selftest-only-gptfixesachecks-no v0.1.0: Closed on an offline selftest only (GptFixesAChecks); no live proof run.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#598 finding.open finding:LIQUID_THEFT_FIX_TANK_HIDES_ITSELF v0.1.0: RM_LiquidTheft.Witness uses the 3-arg GenSight.LineOfSight, which ends at the tank's Position cell only; the 2x2 RM_LiquidTank's other three cells block sight, so a witness approaching through any other cell never sees it and live L6 caught 0 in a day.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#599 cause.refuted cause:gss-s-power-tap-twin-is-not-affected v0.1.0: GSS's power-tap twin is not affected by the same defect: its clamp is 1x1, and POWER_THEFT A2 caught on the first cycle.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#600 finding.open finding:LIQUID_THEFT_VERIFY_FALSE_ACCUSATION v0.1.0: The theft detector (RM_LiquidTheft.TheftTick) compares snapshots only, so any stock change on a foreign tank (corrosion, heat, its owners' machines) charges goodwill as player theft; a balanced take+put between cycles escapes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#601 finding.claimed_fixed finding:LIQUID_THEFT_VERIFY_FALSE_ACCUSATION v0.1.0: Charges only when PlayerReaches the tank; confirmed by reading that owners' own machines and ruins changed foreign stock. (recorded on pre-landing b7d2d03dd2; landed as bce8fc696030)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:34Z BACKFILL#602 finding.open finding:LIQUID_WAKES_FIX_FOOTPRINTS_GONE_QUIET v0.1.0: fw_live_checks W1_wakes_terrain_walked failed/was unmeasured: the row never stood a pawn on the liquids, wanted RM_Fill_Water where dug water settles to RM_WaterThin, never toggled liquidWakesEnabled or counted wakes. RM_LiquidSurface.LookFor returned null for RM_WaterThin.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#603 cause.refuted cause:stale-row-the-failure-was-not-merely-an v0.1.0: Stale row: the failure was not merely an out-of-date result; a re-run still failed (class HARNESS+MOD, not stale).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#604 cause.refuted cause:wakes-truly-off-in-the-mod-the-later v0.1.0: Wakes truly off in the mod: the later live run found the mod looks right; both causes were HARNESS (sample budget 240 ticks cannot cover pathCost 200 slime / 300 tar; empty first Render snapshot parsed as None). Slime ON counted 2 wakes, OFF 0.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#605 trap.noted trap:w1-pawn-paths-around-the-5-row-strips v0.1.0: W1 pawn paths AROUND the 5-row strips (z=41) and takes one position snapshot after order_pawn, so it never stood on any liquid in any run; a FAIL/pass there says nothing about wakes.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#606 trap.noted trap:max-samples-80-x-sample-ticks-3-240 v0.1.0: MAX_SAMPLES=80 x SAMPLE_TICKS=3 = 240 ticks cannot cover pathCost 200 (slime) / 300 (tar); pawn sat on Soil and never entered tar, so the row reads UNMEASURED not mod-broken.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#607 finding.open finding:MAP_STATICS_PRUNE_GHOST_MAPS v0.1.0: Static per-Map tables (RM_LiquidNet dictionaries, RM_PitPathing NativeArray grids, RM_DigDiscovery poolMap) and RM_LiquidSurface pooled meshes keep removed maps alive; pruning only happened on a cache miss, meshes were never destroyed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#608 finding.claimed_fixed finding:MAP_STATICS_PRUNE_GHOST_MAPS v0.1.0: RM_MapComponent_FlowStatics prunes net state/rotation/hose links (C1-F2), disposes pit grids+ladders+entry cache (C3-F2), unpins dig pool (C5-F3) on MapRemoved and FinalizeInit; RM_LiquidSurface.MapRemoved destroys pooled meshes (C5-F4). (recorded on pre-landing df46b3e3a9; landed as 1d77c1765101)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#609 finding.open finding:PANNING_SPOT_VERIFY_EMPTY_RIVER v0.1.0: Panning keeps being offered after every placer rock is mined (stock > 0, pool empty): ShouldSkip reads stock only, while Draw returns null when the pool is empty.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#610 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PANNING_SPOT_VERIFY_EMPTY_RIVER v0.1.0: ShouldSkip now also needs PoolAny (re-measured at most every 2500 ticks), so panning is no longer offered once the pool is mined out. (recorded on pre-landing 0377b63928; landed as 17a082d2d09a)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#611 finding.open finding:PIPE_ADAPTER_FIX_DEBT_NEVER_PAID v0.1.0: Adapter Send mode: on a partial foreign accept, SettleSend refunds the whole unit and records -fraction in carry, but the Send path never consults negative carry (only Take does), so liquid is created.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#612 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIPE_ADAPTER_FIX_DEBT_NEVER_PAID v0.1.0: Negative carry is now charged on the next Send, so partial foreign accepts no longer create liquid. (recorded on pre-landing 3ddf921362; landed as 3f08277146eb)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#613 trap.noted trap:no-live-proof-run-was-done-the-fix v0.1.0: No live proof run was done; the fix is covered only by the offline selftest.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#614 finding.open finding:PIT_ENTRY_PROVE_DONE_BUT_STILL_RED v0.1.0: RM_Ladder and RM_PitRamp are listed broken by mod_completion because WATERED_PIT_FIX_NO_WAY_IN (done) and PIT_CLIMB_PROVE_RAIDERS_VS_SHEER_TRENCH carry a non-pass latest verify; real regression or instrument miscount was unseparated.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#615 trap.noted trap:the-red-status-was-partly-an-instrument-miscount v0.1.0: The red status was partly an instrument miscount in mod_completion, filed as COMPLETION_FIX_PARTIAL_VERIFY_PAINTS_RED; a done item with a non-pass verify is not necessarily a regression.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#616 finding.open finding:PIT_FALL_MEND_SAVED_MIDAIR v0.1.0: A save in the same tick as a jump or forced fall loses the fall damage: RM_SuperdeepTrapState.lastCell is derived, never scribed, and TryJumpInto defers the descent to the next detector pass.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#617 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_FALL_MEND_SAVED_MIDAIR v0.1.0: TryJumpInto resolves the arrival at once by the detector's own test (ResolveArrivalNow -> OnForcedDescent, which marks it handled), so a paused jump saved before unpausing still falls. (recorded on pre-landing 0377b63928; landed as 17a082d2d09a)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#618 finding.open finding:PIT_PRISON_JUDGE_D3_ROOMS v0.1.0: A sheer D3 person-band pocket formed a pit prison room, but capture-down and lip service need D4 (superdeep).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#619 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_PRISON_JUDGE_D3_ROOMS v0.1.0: IsPitCell now requires IsSuperdeepRaw, so a D3 person-band pocket no longer counts as a pit prison room. (recorded on pre-landing 116742f41ac3; landed as 0b459fc8527e)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#620 trap.noted trap:the-selftest-is-source-level-only-and-no v0.1.0: The SelfTest is source-level only and no live D3 check was run, so a D3 prison is not proven live.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#621 finding.open finding:PIT_REACH_VERIFY_FLYERS_AND_GIANTS v0.1.0: Pawnless reachability uses the person band with no flyer exemption, so flyers and K3+ pawns may be refused valid routes; a pawn too wide for its hole reads 'not held' but the cliff masks still wall it in.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:35Z BACKFILL#622 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PIT_REACH_VERIFY_FLYERS_AND_GIANTS v0.1.0: StartPath now records its walker and the veto answers for it (vanilla StartPath ran a pawnless CanReach at person band, C3-F4); masks and step floor step aside for a pawn too wide for its pocket (C3-F5). (recorded on pre-landing 009abe3c49; landed as 9b22587f388a)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#623 finding.open finding:PLACEMENT_FAILURE_MEND_LOST_IN_POSTING v0.1.0: After a debit, TryPlaceThing failures lose the product: reaction item products, fill-from-tank and liquid revert, dig finds merged partly into a stack (not charged to the find budget), and weir catches merged partly (fish created without debiting stock).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#624 finding.claimed_fixed finding:PLACEMENT_FAILURE_MEND_LOST_IN_POSTING v0.1.0: C1-F13 and C2-F6 now place through RM_LiquidBottleUtility.PlacePaidFor (Near, else spawn on the cell; a revert inside a container drops to the ground). C5-F12: the dig find charges the merged share. C6-F9: weir Deposit returns what merged. (recorded on pre-landing 8fdc692b89; landed as b7b7c3b3df58)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#625 finding.open finding:PUMP_POUR_TUNE_NO_FASTER_THAN_GRAVITY v0.1.0: The basic RM_LiquidPump on Pour drains a Dump tank at 5 units/cycle, the same as the gravity rate, although ruling Q3 says pour is the fast drain. Live: 20 vs 25 units per 1000 ticks.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#626 finding.verified finding:PUMP_POUR_TUNE_NO_FASTER_THAN_GRAVITY v0.1.0: The Pour budget is now PourLevels: the basic pump gets 2 levels = 10 units/cycle vs gravity 5, and the stations (2, 4) are unchanged. A failed later level no longer overwrites the inspect status. Live: 30 per 1000 ticks vs 25. (recorded on pre-landing ed20705bd5fa; landed as f239f3745335)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#627 cause.refuted cause:basic-pump-s-levelspercycle-defaults-to-1-no v0.1.0: Basic pump's levelsPerCycle defaults to 1 (no RM_PumpExtension), and one level is 5 units, so Pour moved exactly the gravity rate; the stations at levelsPerCycle 2 and 4 were already faster, so the stations were not the problem.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#628 finding.open finding:REACH_VETO_FIX_ONE_END_ENOUGH v0.1.0: ReachVetoed vetoed only when both ends lacked a way, so a laddered pocket was approved to reach a sealed pocket (and the reverse) and A* then failed, looping jobs on PatherFailed. The map-edge check let a surface island ringed by an unclimbable trench believe it could leave
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#629 finding.claimed_fixed finding:REACH_VETO_FIX_ONE_END_ENOUGH v0.1.0: ReachVetoed now needs a way at both ends (C3-F8, C4-F2); the map-edge veto cuts off a surface island via TouchesEdge (C3-F9); the absolute-rule BFS stops at walls (C3-F12). (recorded on pre-landing 009abe3c49; landed as 9b22587f388a)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#630 finding.open finding:RIVER_CURRENT_PROBE_LATE_GRAB v0.1.0: River steam vents keep puffing from cells that stopped being river (tick never rechecks IsRiver). New river entrants register only every 250 ticks and then wait a full cadence, so a short fast-lane crossing may finish unshoved.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#631 finding.claimed_fixed finding:RIVER_CURRENT_PROBE_LATE_GRAB v0.1.0: A steam vent now puffs only while its cell is still river, checked when due (fixes C6-F10). C6-F6 left unchanged. (recorded on pre-landing 0377b63928; landed as 17a082d2d09a)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#632 trap.noted trap:the-250-tick-scan-gap-c6-f6-was v0.1.0: The 250-tick scan gap (C6-F6) was not fixed: ScanIntervalTicks=250 is the design's sampling, and a crossing shorter than one scan going unshoved is accepted.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#633 finding.open finding:SCENARIO_REELS_FIX_STILL_FACING_WEST v0.1.0: FlowWorks stagers build every hose reel West (RM_HoseReel:x,z,3) though reels turn, so 10 walkthrough hoses leave the west nozzle and U-turn to targets behind the reel.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#634 finding.open finding:SLUICE_GATE_SETTINGS_EXCISE_KNOBS_ON_NOTHING v0.1.0: The FlowWorks 'Sluice gates' settings (on/off and open/shut delays) drove Building_RM_SluiceGate, which no def uses. The player's sluice is RM_Sluice, a door (Building_RM_FlowDoor). Three different things were called 'sluice'.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#635 finding.claimed_fixed finding:SLUICE_GATE_SETTINGS_EXCISE_KNOBS_ON_NOTHING v0.1.0: Deleted Building_RM_SluiceGate, its math and RM_SluiceGateSettings (no def used the class; no caller of RM_SluiceGates outside its own file). Removed the settings section, scribe, site_spec row, validation toggle floor and coverage regex; toggle count 211->210.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#636 finding.open finding:TANK_CONTENTS_SALVAGE_ON_DECONSTRUCT v0.1.0: Deconstructing or destroying a stocked tank silently deletes its contents. CompLiquidConverter has no PostDestroy/PostDeSpawn, so pendingCount is never settled; a drill's pending is lost too.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#637 finding.claimed_fixed finding:TANK_CONTENTS_SALVAGE_ON_DECONSTRUCT v0.1.0: Tank now spills its contents on destroy, drill returns pending to the deposit, converter drops pending items. Adapter carry loss (sub-unit) was left unfixed. (recorded on pre-landing 682b968dc5; landed as 181f26b085bd)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#638 trap.noted trap:verified-only-by-the-offline-selftest-no-live v0.1.0: Verified only by the offline selftest; no live proof was run. The adapter carry loss is sub-unit and was left unfixed.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#639 finding.open finding:TANK_SETTLE_FIX_DOUBLE_DIPPED v0.1.0: A tank that belongs to two net components is settled twice per cycle, breaking the budget and epoch-stock rule. SettleTick builds a view per component from live storedUnits while ComponentsOf(mover) can return several components for one tank. Rate issue, not mass.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#640 finding.claimed_fixed finding:TANK_SETTLE_FIX_DOUBLE_DIPPED v0.1.0: Made the sent/recv budget per tank shared across the components, so a tank in two components is no longer settled twice per cycle. (recorded on pre-landing 72185cbc4c; landed as 121eb90aa35e)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#641 trap.noted trap:verified-only-by-the-offline-selftest-no-live v0.1.0: Verified only by the offline selftest; no live proof was run.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#642 finding.open finding:TESTENV_BUMBLEDRONE_HARDEN_UNINVITED_BUZZ v0.1.0: During FlowWorks extension_proof --site river, a hostile AA_Bumbledrone swarm arrived and stopped the watched wait (GOVERNOR_STOP hostile_arrival); river_site.py applied no quiet-site profile.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#643 finding.verified finding:TESTENV_BUMBLEDRONE_HARDEN_UNINVITED_BUZZ v0.1.0: river_site now removes leftover candidate maps and reads them back (exit 3 if one stays), applies the MAP_ONLY quiet profile with read-back, and loaded_maps reads details.loadedMaps, which is where jawa/set_current_map nests it. (recorded on pre-landing e3f885330d; landed as de853778c885)
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#644 cause.refuted cause:the-hostile-arrival-was-not-on-the-test v0.1.0: The hostile arrival was not on the test map: the triggers carry map uid 3, a leftover rejected river_site candidate (tile 13722), while the test map was uid 9. The swarm had fresh ids, so it was generated then, not at map-gen.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#645 trap.noted trap:river-site-loaded-maps-read-r-get-loadedmaps v0.1.0: river_site.loaded_maps() read r.get('loadedMaps'), but set_current_map nests it under details, so it always returned None and the leftover-candidate drop pass was skipped. The selftest fake world put loadedMaps at top level, so the check could not go red.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#646 trap.noted trap:the-cause-of-the-swarm-s-generation-on v0.1.0: The cause of the swarm's generation on uid 3 is unproven (storyteller was off).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:36Z BACKFILL#647 trap.noted trap:works-ferry-rope-reads-unmeasured-on-the-wide v0.1.0: works/ferry_rope reads UNMEASURED on the wide (size 2.50) river and skips the rest of works; this is a site issue, not this item.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#648 finding.open finding:UNIVERSAL_PIPE_FIX_LAST_RULING_STILL_RED v0.1.0: RM_UniversalPipe is listed broken by mod_completion because HOSE_RULINGS_BUILD_THREE_REVERSALS_ONE_CARD criterion A5's latest verify is not a pass, though that item reads validated.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#649 trap.noted trap:the-previous-a5-verify-10-23-was-partial v0.1.0: The previous A5 verify (10-23) was partial: SL4 UNMEASURED/SITE, and realflow stopped on a downed colonist (HARNESS); neither was a mod fault.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#650 trap.noted trap:liquid-ledger-a4-ns-intake-needs-pump-has v0.1.0: LIQUID_LEDGER A4 (NS_intake_needs_pump) has no live driver, and none was found for A2 (canal from lake) or A8 (worst tick).
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#651 finding.open finding:WEIR_POOL_DIAGNOSE_DAMMED_AT_THE_EDGE v0.1.0: ProofWeirPool returned POOL cells=0 at a weir at z=12 on tile 825, so works.weir_arrest_and_pool failed and 8 later components were UNMEASURED. RM_Building_BankWeir.AddPoolCells found no upstream cell.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#652 cause.refuted cause:the-weir-sat-at-the-map-edge-findweirsite v0.1.0: The weir sat at the map edge: FindWeirSite already skips a margin of 12 cells and z=12 is the first allowed row. Earlier runs pooled at that row (cells 14, 16, 23); only the pre-margin run at (79,0,0) failed from the edge.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#653 cause.refuted cause:the-harness-placed-the-site-wrongly-extension-proof v0.1.0: The harness placed the site wrongly: extension_proof.py and extensions_rivers.py pass no site, only call ProofWeirPool(arg).

- 2026-11-16T08:32:37Z BACKFILL#655 trap.noted trap:findweirsite-checks-only-that-the-wet-end-has v0.1.0: FindWeirSite checks only that the wet end has flow, never that an upstream exists, so a site can be accepted and pool 0.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:39Z BACKFILL#684 trap.noted trap:moving-the-cap-to-after-spending-changes-the v0.1.0: Moving the cap to after spending changes the idle-burst bound (CapAccrued contract, SequenceFuzz mirror) and is a throughput-semantics change. - 2026-11-16T08:32:42Z BACKFILL#756 finding.open finding:FLOW_ORDER_EXTERNAL_INPUT_1 v0.1.0: Channels with no source (pump, drill or rain fed) did not spread by the flow order, and networks over maxComponentCells=6000 were fixed partitions that never exchanged liquid.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:42Z BACKFILL#757 finding.verified finding:FLOW_ORDER_EXTERNAL_INPUT_1 v0.1.0: Part 1 (c37f05633): driver-raised cells within 5000 ticks (PROVISIONAL) seed the flow order, setting pumpFedSpreadEnabled. Part 2: oversized networks exchange across seams via maxNetworkCells and networkSeamsMoveEnabled. An 80x80 network wets end to end.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:42Z BACKFILL#758 cause.refuted cause:the-first-a4-attempt-s-failure-water-never v0.1.0: The first A4 attempt's failure (water never past the first connector square in 63,000 pulses) was not the network cost: every square between the edge strip and the plot sits in the map-edge SINK band (isSink true at x<=9), which eats the front. The inland-source run wetted all 6400 squares.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:42Z BACKFILL#759 trap.noted trap:a-map-edge-fed-source-is-eaten-by v0.1.0: A map-edge-fed source is eaten by the edge SINK band (x<=9), so a big-network timing needs an inland limitless source. The measured pulse cost, 7.5-7.7 ms per pulse incl. the bridge round trip, is what decides whether the default maxNetworkCells 6000 rises.
- 2026-11-16T08:32:42Z BACKFILL#760 finding.open finding:FLOW_SOURCE_SINK_BUILD_TAPS_FOR_MAPMAKERS v0.1.0: Map makers have no in-game way to place a Source or Sink of any FlowWorks liquid at a set rate. Dev-placed RM_FlowSource/RM_FlowSink objects (not buildable, minimal look) must provide them.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:45Z BACKFILL#817 finding.open finding:RIMMANDRAKE_BUILD_FLOWWORKS_TOSS_FLAME_JOBDEF_STILL_UNKNOWN v0.1.0: The 'toss a flame' order does not exist: sc_toss_flame FAILs with 'Unknown JobDef RM_TossFlame', and TOSS_picked_square_lights_first and TOSS_control_water_twin_stays_dark fail.

- 2026-11-16T08:32:46Z BACKFILL#820 trap.noted trap:the-faults-json-cls-key-was-unread-so v0.1.0: The faults.json 'cls' key was unread, so fix checks were silently skipped, and a Polish lane-1 FAIL blocked the exit.
